| Worst is behind us now. Rogue was shaking worse than all those corpodicks put together, I could tell. But she still said yes in the end. We're going back to Arasaka Tower and this time… we finish things right. We'll cut Alt loose in the Tower's system and she'll punch our ticket to Mikoshi. Fuck, how I wish you could see it! Raw direct references, actor bindings, choices, event locstrings and community entries remain in the evidence shards.

## Required acceptance cases before reuse

Exercise normal completion and each named alternate outcome; interrupt and return at every scene handoff; start with spatial/item/actor predicates already satisfied; save/load across waits and joins; cancel parallel listeners; verify reward delivery once; and verify cleanup without changing the committed outcome. Apply the block-specific cases in the [expanded contracts](../all-quest-blocks.md). No installed playtest is claimed.

## Connected paths and supporting bindings

890 repeated typed/socket path signatures in this selected graph context; 140 distinct direct resource identities; 73 embedded graph handoffs. The full occurrence lists, concrete parameters, exact source pointers and external edges are retained in the checked evidence. Shared forward dependencies can serve several quests; these are context counts.
